## Pitfalls

Each is a wrong belief and its correction, deduped across the lessons' "Watch out" sections. Almost every one is a form of [the two symmetrical errors](#the-two-symmetrical-errors); the first two groups state them directly.

### Trap: Inflating an application into a principle

- "The Church teaches this bill, budget, figure or tax" (dignity requires this housing programme; the common good requires this budget; solidarity requires Measure 7): the principle fixes the end and rules some means out; the instrument is an application, and no one may claim the Church's authority for his own solution (GS 43). *([1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md), [3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md), [3.2](lessons/03-02-the-common-good.md), [3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md), [3.4](lessons/03-04-solidarity.md), [3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- A principle's rung passes to its application: an application inherits direction, not weight. *([1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md))*
- A fact stated in an encyclical becomes doctrine: CA 3 denies definitive force to its own reading of events; an empirical premise acquires no rung from its setting (CA 35's efficiency claim; MH 71's platforms; MH 150's "can" deskill). *([1.3](lessons/01-03-see-judge-act.md), [3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md), [4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md), [4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- EG 54's trickle-down verdict binds as doctrine: it has no rung and an economist may test it; the moral claim beside it is a principle. *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- A living-wage figure, allowance amount or minimum is Catholic teaching: application, no rung; preferring another after weighing is not dissent. *([4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md))*
- "QA requires a corporative constitution": QA 82's "must" carries principles through a proposal whose form QA 86 leaves free; later popes kept the bodies, not the blueprint (MM 37). *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- "Leo told Catholics to form Catholic unions", so that is doctrine: §54 is conditional advice ("Under these circumstances"). *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- RN requires, or forbids, a statutory minimum wage: §45 prefers boards and keeps the State as a backstop; the means are application. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- The social Catholics' guilds and cooperatives were Church teaching: no magisterial level; even QA's vocational orders are an application. *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- Acts 4 binds Christians to common ownership: that inflates one community's free practice (Acts 5:4). *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- PT, or CiV 67, asks for a world superstate: consent, not force (PT 138); subsidiarity (PT 140-141); law, subsidiarity, solidarity and universal recognition remain in CiV 67. A design treated as doctrine inflates. *([2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md), [6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- MM's farm programme, or PT's hope for the UN, binds as Catholic policy: application by its own words (MM 126, 142). *([2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md))*
- The priority of labour settles the forms of ownership: LE 14's joint ownership and shareholding are proposals LE leaves to concrete judgment. *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md))*
- QA 65 or LE 14 requires co-ownership: QA 65 ties partnership to present conditions and to what is possible; LE 14 leaves open whether each proposal can be applied. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- CiV's praise of the economy of communion binds Catholic owners to adopt it: an application. *([4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- MH obliges every firm to match each automation with job guarantees: §156 is one viable path beside "no single model" (§153). MH 152 is no ban on automation either. *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md), [4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- "LS teaches a carbon tax", or a Catholic must back whatever LS 165-173 or LD 59 proposes: applications; weigh them, then judge. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- "The Church teaches open borders", the four verbs are doctrine, every visa rule is a sin: inflates the welcome into a ban on regulation. *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- Francis's 2025 letter made opposing one country's programme a duty of faith: its judgment on the programme is an application, and §§4-5 affirm action against serious criminals and orderly legal migration. *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- Distributism is Catholic economics; RN 46 makes it doctrine: RN 46 teaches a direction and leaves the means. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- The Church teaches that democracy is the only legitimate regime: that inflates CA 46's conditional "inasmuch as"; no form is prescribed (GS 74; *Immortale Dei* 4). *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- DH requires a secular or separationist state: DH 6 permits special recognition, on condition of freedom for all. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- Any reading presented as "simply what the Church teaches": each reading has no magisterial level, and its programme is an application (GS 43). *([6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*

### Trap: Demoting a principle to a preference

- "Prudential" means "optional": the applications ask serious consideration and DV 24 makes loyalty the rule. You may end by disagreeing; you may not skip the weighing. *([1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md))*
- GS 43 licenses any disagreement: its freedom covers concrete solutions, not principles, and the same paragraph forbids claiming the Church's authority for your own view. *([1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md))*
- "No model" means the Church has nothing to say about economics: the disclaimers sit beside a claimed competence (GS 76); the Church disclaims the blueprint, not the judgment. Mirror: "the Church's model is X". *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md))*
- "Doctrine concerns faith and morals, so wages and markets are outside it": social doctrine belongs to moral theology (SRS 41). Technical expertise is what the corpus disclaims (CiV 9); moral competence is what it claims. *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md), [4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- OA 4, MH 27 or EG 184 disclaim principles: they disclaim a blueprint; MH 46 and CA 3 keep the principles fixed. *([1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md), [2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- See-judge-act means the teaching follows the data; "the evidence is contested, so the principle lapses": data enter at SEE and revise applications, not principles. *([1.3](lessons/01-03-see-judge-act.md))*
- "Dignity is an empty word", the common good "a pious slogan", subsidiarity "one opinion about federalism", solidarity "a feeling", the option "a 1970s slogan", integral development "1960s economics": each demotes a principle restated across decades. *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md), [3.2](lessons/03-02-the-common-good.md), [3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md), [3.4](lessons/03-04-solidarity.md), [3.6](lessons/03-06-the-preferential-option-for-the-poor.md), [6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- The just wage is a pious hope economics outgrew: the floor and the measures are restated from 1891 to 2026. *([4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md))*
- The priority of labour is a slogan: it has teeth (productive property's only title is service to labour, LE 14). *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md))*
- Since Leo's Catholic unions are application, so is the right to unionize: §51's natural right stands apart from §54's advice. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- "QA is a corporatist period piece": drops subsidiarity, ownership's two sides and social justice with the cart. *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- "Pastoral" means "not binding": GS's note says it rests on doctrinal principles; the note sorts elements, not parts, and GS 80 is in Part Two. *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- "Ambrose was only preaching, so it binds no one": the sermon has no rung, but the principle PP 23 draws from it does. *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- Alms are charity, so optional: giving from surplus, and to one in extreme need, is a precept (q.32 a.5). *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- Property's social function is papal opinion: MH 66 rules that out in so many words. *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- RN 46 is a Victorian aside: QA 59-63, MM 113 and GS 71 restate it. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- "Someone must answer" is a pious hope for engineers, or MH 98 means none of it binds: §98 dates statements about AI, not principles about persons. *([4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- Migration is pure policy, or the state may set whatever conditions it likes: drops two rights and "for the common good". *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- Integral ecology is "the Pope's opinion on science": the principles on creation, the poor's water and future generations hang on no temperature. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- Aid is charity no nation owes: PP 44 and 49 and SRS 32 make it a duty binding nations. *([6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*

### Trap: Misstating a level

- An encyclical is infallible, or, being old, only one pope's opinion: rung 3 by genre, defining nothing; repetition weighs within the rung. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md), [4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- Every "social encyclical" is an encyclical: OA is an apostolic letter; *Laudate Deum* and *Dilexi Te* are exhortations; *Justice in the World* is a synod's text under conditional papal approval. *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md), [6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- "The *Compendium* says so" or "the Catechism says so" fixes a level: both locate; each sentence keeps its source's weight. Mirror: "only a summary, so it weighs nothing". *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md), [1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md), [3.2](lessons/03-02-the-common-good.md), [5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md), [5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- A conference letter binds every Catholic, or no one: authentic in its territory, never universal, and it cannot move a universal level. *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md), [1.2](lessons/01-02-permanent-principles-and-contingent-applications.md), [5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*
- A papal interview outranks a conference letter: an interview is not a teaching act. *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md))*
- *Mater et Magistra* made see-judge-act binding: a method commended, with no rung. *([1.3](lessons/01-03-see-judge-act.md), [2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md))*
- MH made indelible dignity definitive, or a dicastery's declaration is only an opinion: an encyclical raises the grade within rung 3, and an approved declaration is a rung-3 act. *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md))*
- Every item in *Dignitas Infinita*'s catalogue binds at one level: each keeps the weight of what it restates. *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md))*
- QA defined that no socialist can be Catholic, or it was "1931 politics": rung 3 at a heavy grade, no defining formula, judging socialism as doctrine and system. *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md), [4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- Writing "rung 3" for QA 65 or QA 81-87 without saying the assent attaches to the principle, not the proposal. *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- The "constitutive" sentence is papal teaching or a definition; "Vatican II called justice constitutive": a synod's hedged sentence, and its meaning is rung 5. Mirror: JW is not magisterium, so its subject is optional (the floor is EN 29 and CA 5). *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- *Evangelii Nuntiandi* defined "integral", or the International Theological Commission settled it: EN never uses the word "constitutive", and the Commission is advisory. *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- Medellín made the option Church teaching: it never uses the phrase; a regional assembly is not the universal magisterium. *([3.6](lessons/03-06-the-preferential-option-for-the-poor.md))*
- Rome condemned liberation theology in 1984, or LN is infallible, or a dead letter: LN addressed "certain forms" and named no one; Francis and Leo XIV quote it approvingly. *([3.6](lessons/03-06-the-preferential-option-for-the-poor.md))*
- *Antiqua et Nova* is papal teaching: a dicastery note approved in ordinary form. *([4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- DH defined religious liberty, or *Quanta Cura* was plainly not *ex cathedra*: DH is rung 3; QC is a disputed candidate, and "not manifest" is not "plainly not". *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- DH binds no one's mind: DH 2 is rung 3, and a reading that refuses it any assent demotes it. *([6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- The just-war conditions are Aquinas's opinion, or they are defined: the article has no magisterial level, CCC 2309 restates its tradition, and no act defines it. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md))*
- CCC 2309's last sentence puts rulers beyond criticism, or a bishop's verdict on a war binds: it assigns competence only. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md))*
- Since 2017 possession is defined as intrinsically evil and every Catholic in a nuclear force is in mortal sin: two addresses define nothing, and mortal sin has its own conditions. Mirror: John Paul II approved deterrence, so Francis gave his view. *([5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*
- The 2016 Appeal abolished just-war teaching: it has no magisterial level. *([5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*

### Trap: Misreading a document

- Quoting CA 42 without its conditions, or as a blessing or a condemnation of capitalism: it answers two senses of the word, and the yes carries the conditions. See [CA 42](#centesimus-annus-42). *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md), [6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- CA 48 condemns the welfare state: it criticizes excesses and abuses, grants that the welfare state answered real poverty, and gives the State duties in the same section. *([4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- "Not the only indicator" makes profit suspect, or CA makes profit the only measure: CA 35 acknowledges profit's legitimate role and denies it is the sole one. *([4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- CiV 66 makes every purchase from a tainted supply chain a sin: it states responsibility; how far a buyer shares another's wrong is cooperation. *([4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- QA endorsed Fascism: drops QA 95's fears. Mirror: QA condemned the system (drops the advantages it credits). *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- QA condemned moderate socialism because it still attacks property: the ground is its concept of society (QA 117-119). *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- Any public ownership is condemned socialism: QA 114 allows certain property to be reserved to the State; LE 14 does not exclude socialization in suitable conditions. *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- RN 19 condemns every strike: CA 14 reads it as condemning class struggle as a method; RN 39 treats strikes as symptoms. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- RN 42's lines on women are permanent principle, or all of 42 is prejudice: split them; the protective principle stays (LE 19), the occupational line is 1891's application. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- LE 7 condemns capitalism as a system: it names a reversal of order that can recur under any programme, collectivism included. *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md))*
- The right to strike is unlimited once a union votes, or merely tolerated: LE 20 recognizes the right and attaches the limits in the same paragraph. *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md))*
- RN 45 is the whole wage teaching: true of the floor; above it QA 72, QA 74 and GS 67 weigh firm, common good and productivity. *([4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md))*
- The family wage is owed in full by every employer, or an allowance excuses paying below the floor: LE 19 offers two routes; the floor binds regardless. *([4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md))*
- QA 64 blessed the wage system without condition: QA 65 advises partnership and QA 72 binds the firm that pleads hardship. *([4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md))*
- PT's rights are borrowed secular liberalism: grounded in nature and natural law, paired with duties, and drawn from Pius XII's 1942 message. *([2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md))*
- PT 159-160 softened the judgment on communism, or made cooperation a duty, or left everything to lay prudence: the philosophy stays condemned, timing is for prudence, and Church authority may judge practical applications. *([2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md), [4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- DR 58's collaboration ban is a permanent principle: a 1937 directive PT 160 handed to prudence. *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- OA reversed QA: a changed application, not a changed principle (OA 26 still forbids Marxist ideology). *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- OA 4 lets local churches make their own principles: communities draw principles from the social teaching; only analysis and options are local. *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- PP 31 opens a door to revolution: the exception sits inside a warning and covers only manifest, long-standing tyranny (CCC 2243). *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- "The Church has no political mission", from GS 42: drops the next sentence, on the task, light and strength her religious mission yields. *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md))*
- Subsidiarity presumes small government, or help licenses absorbing: it binds both ways (MH 69, 70). It governs every higher and lower body and picks no constitution. *([3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md))*
- "Can do" means "is doing"; substitution is first resort or permanent: the test is capacity, and substitution is as brief as possible. *([3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md))*
- Solidarity belongs to the State and subsidiarity to society, or one outranks the other: CiV 58 binds them both ways. *([3.4](lessons/03-04-solidarity.md))*
- SRS 40 makes the Trinity a social blueprint: a horizon that inspires, not a constitution. *([3.4](lessons/03-04-solidarity.md))*
- "Sum" in the definition of the common good sums goods: it sums conditions. CCC 1906 is a locator, and Vatican II did not coin it. *([3.2](lessons/03-02-the-common-good.md))*
- A large referendum majority settles the common good, or Aquinas taught that the person exists for the state: PT 56 and I-II q.21 a.4 ad 3 say otherwise. *([3.2](lessons/03-02-the-common-good.md))*
- Undignified conditions mean undignified people, or lost moral dignity means lost rights: DI 7-8 say otherwise. *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md))*
- "A worker who signed has forfeited his claim": no man has this power over himself (RN 40). *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md))*
- The destination makes everything everyone's, so the poor may simply take: it puts no object at everyone's disposal (*Compendium* 173); extreme necessity is narrow, and non-use cancels no title (QA 47). *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- The Fathers teach that property is theft: they claim use and surplus; sermons have no magisterial level. *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- "Preferential" slides into "exclusive": every act that uses the word shuts that door (Puebla 1145; LC 68; CA 57). *([3.6](lessons/03-06-the-preferential-option-for-the-poor.md))*
- LN's criticism of political readings of Scripture bans their political dimension: the error is exclusivity (X.5). *([3.6](lessons/03-06-the-preferential-option-for-the-poor.md))*
- "AI is not morally neutral" means AI is evil: MH calls technology neither hostile nor evil in itself (§§4, 9); to "disarm" AI is not to reject it (§110). *([4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- Romans 13 makes every command binding, or any law I dislike is unjust: an unjust command does not bind (PT 51), and a measure within the moral order does (GS 74). Refusal is obligatory, not merely permitted (CCC 2242). *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- Any unjust law licenses force: all five conditions of CCC 2243 must hold, and whether they do is an application. *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- Dropping CA 46's conditions, as if a majority makes right, or calling it "one pope's view": it values democracy "inasmuch as" its conditions hold, and the 2002 Note and MH 39 restate it. *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- DH gives all religions equal rights to the truth: the right is an immunity of persons, and there is no right to error (CCC 2108). *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- Religious liberty is a truce, the hypothesis to drop when Catholics are a majority: DH 2 grounds it in nature. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- DH never applied to confessional states: DH 6 addresses them and DH 2 binds any human power. Mirror: DH condemned integralism (DH 1 and 6 say otherwise). *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md), [6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- DH 15's count of constitutions binds belief: an empirical premise, no rung. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- PT 127 abolished defence: drops its object, *ad violata iura sarcienda*; GS 79 §4 kept defence. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md))*
- Augustine says killing in war is morally indifferent: he says the deaths are not *the* evil, and he still requires lawful authority and a right will. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md))*
- MH 192 abolished just war or revised the Catechism, or is just the Pope's opinion: it keeps self-defence "in the strictest sense" and CCC 2309 is unrevised; what it retires is argued in 5.4. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md), [5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*
- *The Challenge of Peace* approves deterrence: its acceptance is strictly conditioned, its own prudential judgment, not binding in conscience, and one conference's. *([5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*
- The qualifier (as far as they are able) makes the duty a matter of taste: it is part of the principle; "able" is not "willing". *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- The right to remain cancels the welcome: FT 129's "until" and MH 81's "complementary" say otherwise. *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- LS defined the climate science, so doubting a projection is dissent: LS 188 disclaims that. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- LS 30 bans private water firms or bottled water; LS 171 forbids carbon trading; LS 159 fixes a discount rate: each overreads a hedged text. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- LS denies human dominion, or integral ecology divinizes the earth: LS 116 keeps dominion as stewardship; LS 90 and 118 reject biocentrism. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- LS 23's "very solid consensus" sentence asserts the human cause: it concerns warming; the human-cause sentence is separate and cites studies. *([6.1](lessons/06-01-integral-ecology-laudato-si.md))*
- CA 33 made open markets doctrine, or PP 59 made them suspect: CA 33 is a hedged application; PP 59 is a principle about unequal consent that settles no tariff. *([6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- CiV 60 sets an aid figure: it names none. Integral development is anti-growth: SRS 28 keeps the economic dimension. *([6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- Chesterton's "Capitalism" is what CA 42 accepts: he means wage-dependence on a small owning class. Same word, different referents. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- Belloc's reading of social insurance as servility is the Church's: LE 19 and CA 34 list such insurance among workers' rights. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*

### Trap: The natural-right tension

- "Rerum Novarum repeats Aquinas", or "Leo invented a right Aquinas denied": for Aquinas the division of possessions is a human addition to natural law (q.66 a.2 ad 1); RN 6 says "by nature". Their reconciliation is the theologians' (**rung 4**). *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md), [2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md), [3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- Never write "Aquinas taught a natural right to private property". *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- RN's property argument is Locke's: the labour argument (§§9-10) resembles Locke's, but it is "further proof" beside reason, foresight and the family, and use stays common (§22). *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md))*
- FT 120's "secondary" is a revocable concession by the state: that overstates it. Whether it changes RN is **rung 5**; the floor for every reading is that property is a natural right **and** subordinate. *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- "Secondary natural right" comes from the *Compendium*: it is FT 120. *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- "As common" in q.66 a.2 means the poor may take: a.2 grounds the owner's duty; only a.7's extreme case grounds taking. *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*

### Trap: The Rerum Novarum translation and other citation slips

- Quoting RN in the vatican.va English: a later revision, under copyright. Quote the 1903 Benziger text, cited by vatican.va section numbers. *([2.1](lessons/02-01-rerum-novarum.md), [4.2](lessons/04-02-the-just-wage.md), [6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- Citing MM by Italian numbers: the course uses the vatican.va English numbers (EN 59 = IT 45; EN 236 = IT 217). *([2.3](lessons/02-03-mater-et-magistra-and-pacem-in-terris.md))*
- Citing "QA 184-186" from CCC 1883's footnote (AAS pages), or "RN 26" from MH 69's note (RN 35 in the vatican.va English). *([3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md))*
- Quoting GS 25 as "origin, subject and end", or QA 79's "gravest evil": the vatican.va English differs; paraphrase and cite. *([3.1](lessons/03-01-human-dignity.md), [3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md))*
- Quoting *Immortale Dei* 4 from vatican.va, whose English drops the proviso: use the 1903 text. *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- Quoting SRS 41's "technical revolutions": a vatican.va slip for *solutiones technicae*; paraphrase "technical solutions". *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md), [6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- Citing EMCC 21's "cf. GS 87" for the right to regulate immigration: GS 87 concerns population policy and internal migration. *([5.5](lessons/05-05-migration.md))*
- Citing Barmby's "Admonition 21" for Gregory's *Pastoral Rule* III.21: that chapter is headed "Admonition 22". *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*

### Trap: Misattributions

- "Social doctrine" is a 1950 coinage: MH 29 dates the title; QA 87's Latin has the words. *([1.1](lessons/01-01-the-social-doctrine-sources-genre-and-claim.md))*
- Social teaching began in 1891: the principles are scriptural, patristic and Thomist, and the movement was half a century old. *([1.4](lessons/01-04-before-1891-the-roots-and-the-social-question.md))*
- Vatican II invented see-judge-act: Cardijn's JOC, then MM 236, then GS 4. *([1.3](lessons/01-03-see-judge-act.md))*
- "Vocational orders" as QA's words: the vatican.va English says "Industries and Professions". *([2.2](lessons/02-02-quadragesimo-anno-and-the-interwar-proposals.md))*
- The "idolatry of the market" is Francis's phrase: it is CA 40 (1991); EG 55 extends it to money. *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- QA 109's phrase is "international imperialism of money": that is PP 26's English. *([4.3](lessons/04-03-socialism-and-liberal-capitalism-each-judged.md))*
- "Development is the new name for peace" as a numbered PP sentence: it is the heading over 76-87 (§87 says it); CA 52 has the sentence form. *([2.4](lessons/02-04-gaudium-et-spes-to-justice-in-the-world.md), [6.2](lessons/06-02-integral-human-development-and-the-international-order.md))*
- "Unemployment is a grave evil" is LE's phrase: it is MH 151 reading LE 18. LE 3 says work is "probably" the essential key; MH 148 drops the hedge. *([4.1](lessons/04-01-work-and-the-worker-laborem-exercens.md), [4.5](lessons/04-05-the-new-things-of-our-time-magnifica-humanitas.md))*
- The social mortgage was coined at Puebla: say "from John Paul II's Puebla address, restated in SRS 42". *([3.5](lessons/03-05-the-universal-destination-of-goods-and-private-property.md))*
- Citing FT 262 for "possession is immoral", or attributing the possession judgment to Leo XIV: it is the 2017 and 2019 addresses; MH never speaks of possession. *([5.4](lessons/05-04-deterrence-and-the-challenge-to-just-war.md))*
- "Hermeneutic of continuity": Benedict XVI's phrase is a hermeneutic of *reform*. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md), [6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- "Too much capitalism does not mean too many capitalists": not in *The Outline of Sanity*; treat it as a later summary. *([6.3](lessons/06-03-distributism-and-the-alternative-proposals.md))*
- Sorting an encyclical by who drafted it (Weigel's gold and red): the act is the Pope's. Mirror: "Francis replaced John Paul II" (MH 39 restates CA). *([6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- "Integralism" without saying which: political integralism is not the anti-modernist "integralism" of `church-history` 8.3. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md), [6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- Filing Vermeule's common-good constitutionalism under integralism: a neighbour; it does not rest on the two powers. *([6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*
- Treating Leo XIV's addresses or MH as taking a reading's side: MH takes none. *([6.4](lessons/06-04-three-readings-catholic-liberal-integralist-progressive.md))*

### Trap: False friends

- Subsidiarity is Burke's little platoons, Hegel's corporations, Tocqueville's associations or the EU's rule under a Latin name: family resemblances. Subsidiarity is normative and two-sided, grounded in the person. *([3.3](lessons/03-03-subsidiarity.md))*
- "Solidarity" means one thing everywhere: Durkheim's is a social fact, Adam and Christ's theological, Wojtyła's a philosophical attitude, SRS's a taught virtue. *([3.4](lessons/03-04-solidarity.md))*
- CA's "human ecology" is Robert George's "moral ecology": neighbours, not synonyms. *([5.1](lessons/05-01-the-political-community-and-the-limits-of-the-state.md))*
- CA 48's Social Assistance State is Tocqueville's "tutelary" power: a family resemblance. *([4.4](lessons/04-04-enterprise-consumption-and-the-economy-of-gift.md))*
- Rawls thinks religion false, public reason is a speech law, or he rests on scepticism: caricatures. *([5.2](lessons/05-02-religious-liberty-dignitatis-humanae.md))*
- Realism is "might makes right": that is Thucydides' Athenians or Thrasymachus, not Morgenthau. *([5.3](lessons/05-03-war-and-peace.md))*
